   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Hex (Trap) Bar Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Pull-up** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Close-Grip Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 115-140 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (about 3 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Nordic Hamstring Curl + Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Machine Calf Raise + Seated DB Lateral Raise (about 5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Lateral Shuffles, Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **V-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=V-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `V-Up`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Single-Leg Broad Jump** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Land and go: stick the landing, then jump again right away; reset between reps
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Broad%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Leg Broad Jump`
2. **Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 14 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Forward%2FBackward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops`
3. **Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops** — 2 × 6 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Alternating%20Back-to-Wall%20Rotational%20Scoops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops`
4. **Lateral Shuffles** — 3 × 10 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Munoz Formation (agility drill pattern) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Krumrie Formation (agility drill pattern) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lateral%20Shuffles) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lateral Shuffles`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

_You can switch off these blocks today: core, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio

**Core**
1. **Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Weighted Sit-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Weighted Decline Sit-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Sit-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Sit-Up`

**Aerobic**
1. **Orange (C): long / easy · run** — 34 min · 11 min run / 2 min walk, repeat
   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.
   - This cardio session is part of the plan even if you skip the optional lifting.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Orange%20(C)%3A%20long%20%2F%20easy%20%C2%B7%20run)

## Week 4 — Driving it

_Week level: 6.7 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Side (Lateral) Lunge (Friday)
- +1 set on Single-Arm Lat Pulldown (Friday)
- +1 set on Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) (Wednesday)
- +1 set on Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (Wednesday)
- +1 set on Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (Monday)

**Athletic work this week:** 96 jump contacts · 70 ankle-hop contacts · 28 throws · 102 direction changes · 400 m of sprints · 5 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 58% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 105-125 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Plank (forearm / low plank) + Copenhagen Plank (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone), Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Depth Jump, Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine)_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Medball Plank Rockers (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Plank (straight-arm plank) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / L-Sit Hold (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Plank%20(forearm%20%2F%20low%20plank)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Plank (forearm / low plank)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 10-30 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 2 × 4 x 20 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Falling-Start Accelerations`
2. **Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Forward Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Forward Plate Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Lateral%20Hurdle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops`
3. **Depth Jump** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - First reactive jumps: two feet, low box, quality over height
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Bilateral Forward Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Hurdle Hops into Box Jump (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Depth%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Depth Jump`
4. **Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)** — 2 × 18 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Ankle%20Hops%20(bilateral%2C%20in%20place)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)`
5. **L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)** — 3 × 4 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - 3 cuts a rep
   - Swap for: Lateral Shuffles (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Hops in Place (foot-speed / reactive hops) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=L-Test%20%2F%20L-Drill%20(3-Cone)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / High-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hang%20Power%20Clean%20-%20from%20the%20High%20Hang%20(hip%20crease)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 3 × 8-10 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Hex (Trap) Bar Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Pull-up** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Close-Grip Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Step-Up%20(forward%20and%20lateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Step-Up (forward and lateral)`

**Accessories**
1. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

### Tuesday · Aerobic day
_Optional: you can switch off this cardio session, or move it to another day._
- **Green (B): moderate · bike** · 26 min · 3 min hard / 3 min easy, repeat
  - Or use another of your cardio tools: run. Go by time, not distance.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 130-155 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (about 3 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Nordic Hamstring Curl + Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Machine Calf Raise + Seated DB Lateral Raise (about 5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Lateral Shuffles, Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **V-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=V-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `V-Up`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Single-Leg Broad Jump** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Land and go: stick the landing, then jump again right away; reset between reps
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Broad%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Leg Broad Jump`
2. **Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 17 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Forward%2FBackward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops`
3. **Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops** — 2 × 7 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Alternating%20Back-to-Wall%20Rotational%20Scoops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops`
4. **Lateral Shuffles** — 3 × 10 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Munoz Formation (agility drill pattern) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Krumrie Formation (agility drill pattern) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lateral%20Shuffles) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lateral Shuffles`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 4 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

**Accessories**
1. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl (keeps the hamstring curl; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Seated Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lying Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Nordic Hamstring Curl`
2. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Delt DB Row (keeps the rear-delt work; loses the same direction of movement) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly`
3. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the top-end load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`
4. **Seated DB Lateral Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / DB Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Barbell Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses each hand moving on its own) / DB Lateral (Side) Raise (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated DB Lateral Raise`

**Aerobic**
1. **Orange (C): long / easy · run** — 36 min · steady pace: you can still talk
   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Orange%20(C)%3A%20long%20%2F%20easy%20%C2%B7%20run)

### Friday · S3 - Olympic + strength
_Average time: about 110-135 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Leg Raise (lying / supine) (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Barbell Bench Press + Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Single-Arm DB/KB Row + Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle, Broad Jump · small-muscle accessories: Barbell Calf Raise_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core**
1. **Side Plank (elbow)** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Star Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Lateral Crunch (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20Plank%20(elbow)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side Plank (elbow)`
2. **Leg Raise (lying / supine)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: V-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Raise%20(lying%20%2F%20supine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Raise (lying / supine)`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed)** — 2 × 3 x 40 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Falling-Start Accelerations (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Acceleration Starts (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Max-Velocity%20Sprint%20(Top%20Speed)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed)`
2. **Broad Jump** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Land and go: stick the landing, then jump again right away; reset between reps
   - Swap for: Single-Leg Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Broad%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Broad Jump`
3. **5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — 3 × 6 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - 2 cuts a rep
   - Swap for: Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Lateral Shuffles (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle) · Name to copy into YouTube: `5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Single-Arm DB Snatch** — 2 × 6/6 · move it fast: if the dumbbell slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Power work: move the dumbbell fast; stop the set if it slows down.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Mid-Hang Snatch Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / High-Hang Snatch Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Staggered-Stance DB Hang Snatch (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20DB%20Snatch) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm DB Snatch`

**Strength**
1. **Barbell Bench Press** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline DB Bench Press - Feet on Wall (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Barbell Bench Press`
2. **Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Barbell Deadlift (conventional) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hex%20(Trap)%20Bar%20Deadlift) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift`
3. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bird Dog Row (DB) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20DB%2FKB%20Row) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm DB/KB Row`
4. **Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-Elevated%20Lunge%20(RFE%20%2F%20Bulgarian%20split%20squat)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)`
5. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Walking Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`
6. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Close Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Lat%20Pulldown) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm Lat Pulldown`

**Accessories**

## Week 5 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 8.9 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Leg Extension (machine) (Monday)
- +1 set on Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) (Monday)
- +1 set on Barbell Bench Press (Friday)

**Athletic work this week:** 102 jump contacts · 80 ankle-hop contacts · 32 throws · 108 direction changes · 440 m of sprints · 6 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 60% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 105-130 min (an estimate, for information only)_

_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (about 5.5 min saved)_

  - Or use another of your cardio tools: run. Go by time, not distance.

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 130-155 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Weighted Russian Twist + V-Up (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops + Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (about 3 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Flat DB Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Nordic Hamstring Curl + Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Machine Calf Raise + Seated DB Lateral Raise (about 5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Lateral Shuffles, Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops, Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly, Nordic Hamstring Curl · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Landmine Rotations - standing or kneeling (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / KB Book Opener (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Break Dancers (bear/crab-position thread-through rotations) (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Kettlebell Rotational Swings (no hip thrust - trunk-driven) (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Russian%20Twist) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Russian Twist`
2. **V-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=V-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `V-Up`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Single-Leg Broad Jump** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Land and go: stick the landing, then jump again right away; reset between reps
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Broad%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Leg Broad Jump`
2. **Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 20 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Forward%2FBackward%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops`
3. **Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops** — 2 × 8 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Alternating%20Back-to-Wall%20Rotational%20Scoops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops`
4. **Lateral Shuffles** — 3 × 12 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Munoz Formation (agility drill pattern) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Krumrie Formation (agility drill pattern) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lateral%20Shuffles) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lateral Shuffles`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 4 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)`

**Accessories**
1. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.

2. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Delt DB Row (keeps the rear-delt work; loses the same direction of movement) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly`
3. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the top-end load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`
4. **Seated DB Lateral Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1-2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / DB Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Barbell Upright Row (keeps the side-delt work; loses each hand moving on its own) / DB Lateral (Side) Raise (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated DB Lateral Raise`

   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**

   - Land and go: stick the landing, then jump again right away; reset between reps
   - Swap for: Single-Leg Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Broad%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Broad Jump`
3. **5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — 3 × 6 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - 2 cuts a rep
   - Swap for: Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Lateral Shuffles (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Eight-Square Plyometric Formation (8-box hop grid) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)

## Week 6 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 11 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**End test this week: each test takes the place of that day's sprint, change-of-direction or jump station** (Speed / agility, Jumps)

_This week jumps more than usual (16 % more sets for Hips (glutes + hamstrings)): adjust if needed._

_Last week: one intensifier on each of these days (Monday and Wednesday): a MYO set on the day's last small-muscle or machine exercise_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (Friday)
- +1 set on Nordic Hamstring Curl (Wednesday)
- +1 set on Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift (Friday)
- +1 set on Single-Arm DB/KB Row (Friday)
- +1 set on Flat DB Bench Press (Wednesday)
- +1 set on Machine Calf Raise (Wednesday)

**Athletic work this week:** 48 jump contacts · 38 ankle-hop contacts · 20 throws · 60 direction changes · 240 m of sprints · 5 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 58% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 100-125 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Plank (forearm / low plank) + Copenhagen Plank (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Step-Up (forward and lateral) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place), Depth Jump · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine)_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Medball Plank Rockers (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Plank (straight-arm plank) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / L-Sit Hold (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Plank%20(forearm%20%2F%20low%20plank)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Plank (forearm / low plank)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 10-30 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Test**
1. **Test: 10-30 m sprint** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
2. **Test: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Forward Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Forward Plate Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Lateral%20Hurdle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops`
2. **Depth Jump** — 3 × 3 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - First reactive jumps: two feet, low box, quality over height
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Bilateral Forward Hurdle Hops (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Hurdle Hops into Box Jump (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Bilateral Lateral Plate Hops (Forward On / Sideways Off-and-On / Forward Down, Repeat) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Depth%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Depth Jump`
3. **Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)** — 2 × 10 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Ankle%20Hops%20(bilateral%2C%20in%20place)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / High-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hang%20Power%20Clean%20-%20from%20the%20High%20Hang%20(hip%20crease)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 4 × 8-10 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Hex (Trap) Bar Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Pull-up** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Close-Grip Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Step-Up%20(forward%20and%20lateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Step-Up (forward and lateral)`

**Accessories**
1. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set: about 15 reps, then 5 mini-sets of 5 reps with a short pause (about 5 breaths) between, same weight · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`
